# A8 perf baseline audit (Wave 4 Stage A close)

You are codex-perf-auditor running the Wave 4 Stage A8 perf baseline. Repo at `/home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb`.

## Goal

Produce a perf baseline measurement for `apps/site` post-Stage-A (A1-A7 merged). The output informs A8's data-driven chunking decision per ADR-0011 D10:
- IF `@skb/heavy-block-boundary` code is leaking into prose-only chunks → recommend ADD `manualChunks` rule
- ELSE → recommend NO-OP (current chunking is sufficient)

## Inputs

1. `apps/site/astro.config.mjs` — current chunking (post-A5: 4 chunks for heavy blocks + heavy-boundary-dimensions)
2. `packages/heavy-block-boundary/` — the boundary package source (~150 LOC TSX + ~95 LOC CSS + ~390 LOC tests)
3. `apps/site/src/components.ts` — apps/site consumer with HeavyBlockBoundary factories (post-A5)
4. `apps/site/src/pages/sample-blocks-astro.astro` — A7's direct .astro consumer page

## Tasks

1. **Run `pnpm --filter @skb/site build`** to produce dist/ output. Expect chunks for block-jupyter, block-nn-viz, block-agent-flow, heavy-boundary-dimensions, plus prose-only chunks for index/notes/search routes.

2. **Bundle size analysis**: 
   - List `apps/site/dist/_astro/*.js` chunks with sizes (in bytes + KB)
   - Identify which chunks contain `heavy-block-boundary` code (grep the chunks for `HeavyBlockBoundary` symbol or `heavy-block-skeleton` class names)
   - For each chunk containing heavy-block-boundary code, check if it's ALSO containing prose-only route code (notes/[...slug] or index)
   - **Key finding**: does heavy-block-boundary leak into prose-only chunks?

3. **Lighthouse score** (if feasible — may need playwright + lighthouse-ci):
   - Run `pnpm --filter @skb/site exec astro build && pnpm --filter @skb/site exec astro preview --host 127.0.0.1 --port 4321 &`
   - Wait for server ready
   - Skip Lighthouse if no headless browser available; document baseline as N/A
   - Stop the preview server

4. **Astro build output**: capture stdout from `pnpm --filter @skb/site build` showing route → chunk mapping if Astro reports it

5. **Decision recommendation**:
   - **CHUNKED**: if heavy-block-boundary leaks into prose-only chunks → recommend adding `if (id.includes('heavy-block-boundary')) return 'heavy-block-boundary';` rule before existing rules in apps/site/astro.config.mjs
   - **NO-OP**: if heavy-block-boundary stays only in heavy block chunks (because consumers transitively bring it in via the block chunks) → recommend NO-OP

## Heavy-block-boundary leak analysis

- `rg -n '"'heavy-block-skeleton|heavy-block-boundary|HeavyBlockBoundary' apps/site/dist"'` found matches only in:
  - `apps/site/dist/_astro/_slug_.BqnIxVPD.css`
  - `apps/site/dist/notes/sample-blocks/index.html`
- No matches were found in any `apps/site/dist/_astro/*.js` file.
- Route-to-chunk evidence from generated HTML indicates:
  - `index` and `sample-blocks-astro` and all `notes/[...slug]` prose routes consistently load `client.nc8uITnr.js` + `design-tokens.CGGd5A6H.js` (+ `index.BJAJaQ1G.css`).
  - `search` additionally loads `SearchBox.astro_astro_type_script_index_0_lang.D6M5dpgC.js`.
  - Only the notes routes that include heavy-boundary placeholders (`notes/sample-blocks/index.html`) include heavy-boundary CSS class names in markup/CSS.
- Heavy-block-boundary content is not observed in prose-only chunks for `/` or `/search`.

Given current artifact state, `heavy-block-boundary` is not leaking into prose-only JS chunks; observed heavy-boundary-specific markers are only in notes markdown route-specific CSS/markup, not in the route JS bundles listed above.
